# Fable whole-branch review reply (raw, retained), range 6038c37..6c38ec9, 2026-09-13

### Strengths

- The removal is the right shape and honours the reparse invariant at every level. `tools/review-tree-removal.ps1:78-83` deletes a directory link with the non-recursive `Directory.Delete` and a file link with `File.Delete`, never descending; `:146-149` refuses a root that is itself a reparse point; the callers run the ancestor guard first (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:114-119`, `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1799`). Group 1 drives the intact-junction, dangling-junction and link-root cases against real junctions (`evals/multi-model-verify/test_mirror_reaper.py:137-208`).
- Item 98 is genuinely closed: the postcondition is read back rather than inferred (`tools/review-tree-removal.ps1:167-179`), the mirror tool terminates before `New-Item` on a named failure (`tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:2007-2013` vs `:2015`), and the test drives a real held handle rather than a stub (`evals/multi-model-verify/test_review_mirror.py:3237-3256`).
- The emitter's ordering is correct for the two invariants that matter: every reap refusal runs before `$attDir` is created (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:216-232` precedes `:235`), a failed or absent record write exits 2 with "nothing was reaped" (`:282-291`), and the reap runs only after `attestation written:` (`:292-325`). The parametrised wrong-tree case checks the record is absent for all eight shapes (`test_mirror_reaper.py:313-346`).
- The git identity reads are pinned to the tree's own git dir (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:153`, `:162-164`), so an invalid `.git` directory cannot borrow a parent repository's HEAD; `test_mirror_reaper.py:451-461` proves it. The remediation shape is checked exactly as specified (author email, parent count 2, parent 1 equals head), and I confirmed the mirror tool commits with `-c user.email=parallax@local` (`tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:2216`), which sets the author email `%ae` reads.
- `$MirrorPath` is already absolute (`tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1250`) when the `-Force` path hands it to `Remove-ReviewTree`, so the .NET `GetFullPath` cwd trap does not apply there; the emitter refuses a non-rooted path outright (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:82-85`).
- The M5 rename is complete: no `kerev` spelling survives under `skills/`, `agents/`, `commands/`, `tools/`, `evals/` or the backlog.
- The M3 fix names the unattempted bridge on a mirror failure (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:297`, pinned at `test_mirror_reaper.py:363`), and the CI module list carries the new module under both hosts with a self-check (`test_mirror_reaper.py:211-221`).

### Issues

#### Critical

None.

#### Important

1. **The spec's central claim does not hold for plan-mode debates, and neither the spec nor item 101 says so.** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper-design.md:44-47` says the attestation is "the one terminal event the plugin already records mechanically" and no sweep exists; `skills/multi-model-verify/SKILL.md:378` restricts the emitter to "Mode diff only". A plan-mode debate builds the same mirror through preflight 3 and has no attestation, so its mirror has no reap point at all. The only route is the hand removal the doctor names (`commands/doctor.md:387`), which is the prose-rule mechanism the spec rejects at `:29-31`. `BACKLOG.md:177-186` ("What closing it means") would close item 101 against a mechanism that covers one of the two debate modes. This is a record defect, not a code defect: state the plan-mode residual in the spec and in item 101 or 102 (one sentence each), so the closure and the doctor's "removed by hand" branch describe the same population.

2. **The full gate at the branch head is not on record.** The plan's Global Constraints (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper.md:20`) require the six CI commands before the final commit. The ledger's last evidence is "230 passed both hosts" at e3818f9 (`.superpowers/sdd/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper/progress.md:30`); commit 6c38ec9 then edited `BACKLOG.md` (items 101 and 102, with `Verified:` digests at `BACKLOG.md:98` and `:144`), and nothing records `backlog_lint.py` or the rest of the gate against that head. Gap, not a finding against the code: run the six commands at 6c38ec9 and record the result in the ledger before the debate treats the digests as verified.

#### Minor

1. **Sidecar failure omits the unattempted bridge (M3 residual).** `tools/write-attestation.ps1:315-317` exits 3 when the sidecar cannot be deleted, but its message does not carry the "; the bridge was not attempted: <path>" note that `Invoke-Reap` adds at `:297`. A sidecar failure with a bridge named leaves the bridge on disk with no message saying so. One-line fix: append the same `$notAttempted` text.

2. **Rule 5 accepts a `.git` that is a junction.** The spec requires a `.git` DIRECTORY (`spec:63-65`); the check at `tools/write-attestation.ps1:148` tests only the Directory bit, which a junction also carries, so the HEAD read at `:153` goes through the link and the identity is borrowed from the target. The removal itself is safe (the junction is deleted as a link, `review-tree-removal.ps1:78-80`), so the damage is bounded to the named tree's own files, and it is the item 102 class of "the session named the wrong tree". Adding a ReparsePoint test on `$ga` costs one line.

3. **Three guard branches have no test.** The bridge's `$allowRemediation = $false` path (`tools/write-attestation.ps1:161`, a bridge carrying a `parallax@local` commit above the head must be refused; `test_mirror_reaper.py:298-309` covers only a stale head), the read-only ROOT branch (`review-tree-removal.ps1:158-161`; `test_mirror_reaper.py:466-473` sets `+R` on a subdirectory only), and the non-rooted path refusal (`write-attestation.ps1:82-85`). None is a correctness risk on the range; they are the parts of the guard the cross-vendor reviewer will ask about.

4. **Behavioral evals not recorded for the SKILL.md edit.** `CLAUDE.md` names `run_behavioral_evals.py --changed` for skill/prompt changes; the branch removes two Common-mistakes bullets (`skills/multi-model-verify/SKILL.md:411-415`) and inserts a paragraph at `:389-390`. The ledger records skill_lint and the pin suites only. Opt-in by policy, so a gap to name rather than a defect.

5. **An ESCALATE that the user may still extend is not terminal, and the prose does not say so.** `skills/multi-model-verify/references/preflight-mirror.md:93-99` binds the reap to the attestation; the 0.34.0 debate escalated at round 4 and the user extended by two rounds before attestation. A session that emits an ESCALATE attestation with `-ReapMirror` before the user declines to extend turns the extension into a transport failure on `resume`. One clause in the End of life section ("emit the attestation only once the user has declined to extend") closes it.

### Ledger minors triage

- **Task 1: plan cites :1791-1819, actual extent 1791-1820** (`progress.md:16`) - ride. A citation in the plan; the diff shows the whole block removed and re-created verbatim.
- **Task 3: no test for mirror-equals-bridge refusal** (`progress.md:21`) - ride, already closed. `test_overlapping_mirror_and_bridge_are_refused[same]` (`test_mirror_reaper.py:477-490`) drives exactly that shape against the rewritten overlap check at `write-attestation.ps1:225-232`.
- **Task 3: sidecar failure path repeats Invoke-Reap's message format inline** (`progress.md:22`) - fix before merge, because it is now also where Minor 1 lives: routing the sidecar through the same message builder removes the duplication and the missing "not attempted" note in one edit.
- **Task 4: End of life section and doctor check do not mention the .source-manifest sidecar** (`progress.md:26`) - ride. The spec states it (`spec:94-95`), the emitter prints `reaped sidecar:` (`write-attestation.ps1:319`), and the reference is about the guard rather than the inventory.
- **Final fix wave: Group 4 comment omits M3; M4 sentence is one long line** (`progress.md:31`) - ride both. The comment at `test_mirror_reaper.py:437` is a label; the long line at `preflight-mirror.md:115` carries pinned anchors, and the no-reflow constraint makes leaving it the safer choice.

   6: 
   7: **Architecture:** One dot-sourced function file, `tools/review-tree-removal.ps1`, holds the mirror tool's existing directory-link guard (moved, not copied) and the new explicit post-order removal `Remove-ReviewTree`. `tools/write-attestation.ps1` gains `-ReapMirror` and `-ReapBridge`, validates both against the reviewed repo and the attested head BEFORE writing the record, and removes them AFTER. `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1` uses the same function for `-Force` and names the reap route in its existing-path refusal. `commands/doctor.md` gains an inventory check. A new pytest module drives all of it under both PowerShell hosts.

  37: - [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**
  38: 
  39: Create `evals/multi-model-verify/test_mirror_reaper.py`:
  40: 
  41: ```python
  42: """The review mirror reaper (BACKLOG items 101 and 98; spec
  43: docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper-design.md).
  44: 
  45: Four groups. REMOVAL: tools/review-tree-removal.ps1's Remove-ReviewTree,
  46: driven through a harness that dot-sources it, removes a tree with
  47: read-only files and a junction whose target survives, and reports a
  48: named failure on a held handle, a missing path and a link. EMITTER:
  49: tools/write-attestation.ps1's reap parameters refuse every wrong tree
  50: before writing the record and remove the right ones after. MIRROR: the
  51: mirror tool's rebuild removal goes through the same function and its
  52: existing-path refusal names the reap route. PROSE: the skill, the
  53: reference and the doctor carry the rule.
  54: 
  55: WINDOWS ONLY: junctions and the held-handle sharing rule are Windows
  56: semantics, and the mirror tool is a Windows tool. The powershell-hosts
  57: CI job runs this module under BOTH powershell.exe and pwsh.exe; a green
  58: run on one host proves ONE interpreter.
  59: """
  60: import os
  61: import re
  62: import shutil
  63: import subprocess
  64: from pathlib import Path
  65: 
  66: import pytest
  67: 
  68: REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
  69: REMOVAL = REPO / "tools" / "review-tree-removal.ps1"
  70: WRITE = REPO / "tools" / "write-attestation.ps1"
  71: MIRROR_TOOL = REPO / "tools" / "new-review-mirror.ps1"
  72: SKILL = REPO / "skills" / "multi-model-verify" / "SKILL.md"
  73: PREFLIGHT = REPO / "skills" / "multi-model-verify" / "references" / "preflight-mirror.md"
  74: DOCTOR = REPO / "commands" / "doctor.md"
  75: 
  76: POWERSHELL = (os.environ.get("PARALLAX_PS_HOST")
  77:               or shutil.which("powershell") or shutil.which("pwsh"))
  78: 
  79: pytestmark = pytest.mark.skipif(
  80:     os.name != "nt" or POWERSHELL is None,
  81:     reason="the reaper removes junction-bearing trees under a Windows "
  82:            "PowerShell host")
  83: 
  84: 
  85: def run_ps(script, *args):
  86:     return subprocess.run(
  87:         [POWERSHELL, "-NoProfile", "-NonInteractive", "-ExecutionPolicy",
  88:          "Bypass", "-File", str(script), *args],
  89:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=180)
  90: 
  91: 
  92: def git(repo, *args):
  93:     return subprocess.run(
  94:         ["git", "-C", str(repo), "-c", "user.name=reap-test",
  95:          "-c", "user.email=t@localhost", *args],
  96:         check=True, capture_output=True, text=True).stdout.strip()
  97: 
  98: 
  99: def make_repo(tmp_path, name="repo"):
 100:     """A repo with a base commit and a feature commit, returned with both
 101:     SHAs, because an attestation needs a real base..head range."""
 102:     repo = tmp_path / name
 103:     repo.mkdir()
 104:     subprocess.run(["git", "init", "-q", "-b", "main"], cwd=repo,
 105:                    check=True, capture_output=True)
 106:     (repo / "a.txt").write_text("a\n", encoding="utf-8")
 107:     git(repo, "add", "a.txt")
 108:     git(repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "base")
 109:     base = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
 110:     git(repo, "checkout", "-q", "-b", "feat")
 111:     (repo / "b.txt").write_text("b\n", encoding="utf-8")
 112:     git(repo, "add", "b.txt")
 113:     git(repo, "commit", "-q", "-m", "feat")
 114:     head = git(repo, "rev-parse", "HEAD")
 115:     return repo, base, head
 116: 
 117: 
 118: def make_mirror(repo, path):
 119:     """A mirror the way the mirror tool makes one: a FILE COPY that keeps
 120:     .git, so it carries git's read-only object files."""
 121:     shutil.copytree(repo, path)
 122:     return path
 123: 
 124: 
 125: def make_bridge(repo, path, branch="feat"):
 126:     """A clone bridge the way a KitnEssentials session makes one."""
 127:     subprocess.run(["git", "clone", "-q", "--no-checkout", str(repo), str(path)],
 128:                    check=True, capture_output=True)
 129:     git(path, "checkout", "-q", "-b", branch, "origin/" + branch)
 130:     return path
 131: 
 132: 
 133: def junction(link, target):
 134:     subprocess.run(["cmd", "/c", "mklink", "/J", str(link), str(target)],
 135:                    check=True, capture_output=True)
 136: 
 137: 
 138: def read(path):
 139:     assert path.is_file(), f"missing file: {path}"
 140:     return path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
 141: 
 142: 
 143: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 144: # Group 1: Remove-ReviewTree through a dot-sourcing harness
 145: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 146: HARNESS = (
 147:     'param([string]$Target)\n'
 148:     '. "{removal}"\n'
 149:     '$r = Remove-ReviewTree $Target\n'
 150:     'if ($r.Ok) {{ Write-Output "OK" ; exit 0 }}\n'
 151:     'Write-Output ("FAIL: " + $r.Reason)\n'
 152:     'exit 1\n'
 153: )
 154: 
 155: 
 156: def harness(tmp_path):
 157:     h = tmp_path / "harness.ps1"
 158:     h.write_text(HARNESS.format(removal=str(REMOVAL).replace("\\", "/")),
 159:                  encoding="ascii")
 160:     return h
 161: 
 162: 
 163: def test_removal_file_is_ascii_and_defines_nothing_but_functions():
 164:     raw = REMOVAL.read_bytes()
 165:     raw.decode("ascii")
 166:     body = raw.decode("ascii")
 167:     assert "function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink(" in body
 168:     assert "function Remove-ReviewTree(" in body
 169:     # No top-level statement: every non-blank, non-comment line outside
 170:     # a function is a brace or a declaration. A dot-sourced file that
 171:     # RUNS something would run it inside both callers.
 172:     assert "param(" not in body
 173:     assert "Remove-Item -Recurse" not in body and "-Recurse" not in body, (
 174:         "the removal is explicit post-order, never Remove-Item -Recurse")
 175: 
 176: 
 177: def test_removes_a_tree_with_read_only_files_and_keeps_a_junction_target(tmp_path):
 178:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
 179:     tree = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "tree")
 180:     target = tmp_path / "target"
 181:     target.mkdir()
 182:     (target / "keep.txt").write_text("kept\n", encoding="utf-8")
 183:     junction(tree / "linked", target)
 184:     assert (tree / "linked" / "keep.txt").is_file()
 185:     objects = list((tree / ".git" / "objects").rglob("*"))
 186:     assert any(p.is_file() and not os.access(p, os.W_OK) for p in objects), (
 187:         "the fixture must carry a read-only git object")
 188:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(tree))
 189:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 190:     assert proc.stdout.strip() == "OK"
 191:     assert not tree.exists()
 192:     assert (target / "keep.txt").read_text(encoding="utf-8") == "kept\n", (
 193:         "the junction's target must survive the removal")
 194: 
 195: 
 196: def test_a_held_handle_fails_by_name_and_leaves_the_tree(tmp_path):
 197:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
 198:     tree = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "tree")
 199:     held = tree / "held.txt"
 200:     held.write_text("open\n", encoding="utf-8")
 201:     with open(held, "r", encoding="utf-8"):
 202:         proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(tree))
 203:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 204:     assert proc.stdout.startswith("FAIL: "), proc.stdout
 205:     assert str(held) in proc.stdout, (
 206:         "the failure must name the entry that could not be removed")
 207:     assert held.exists() and tree.exists()
 208: 
 209: 
 210: def test_a_missing_path_and_a_file_are_refused(tmp_path):
 211:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(tmp_path / "absent"))
 212:     assert proc.returncode == 1 and "does not exist" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
 213:     f = tmp_path / "plain.txt"
 214:     f.write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
 215:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(f))
 216:     assert proc.returncode == 1 and "is a file, not a tree" in proc.stdout
 217:     assert f.exists()
 218: 
 219: 
 220: def test_a_link_as_the_root_is_refused_and_its_target_survives(tmp_path):
 221:     target = tmp_path / "target"
 222:     target.mkdir()
 223:     (target / "keep.txt").write_text("kept\n", encoding="utf-8")
 224:     link = tmp_path / "link"
 225:     junction(link, target)
 226:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(link))
 227:     assert proc.returncode == 1, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 228:     assert "is a link" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
 229:     assert (target / "keep.txt").is_file()
 230:     assert link.exists(), "a refused removal must not have removed the link either"
 231: 
 232: 
 233: def test_a_filesystem_root_is_refused(tmp_path):
 234:     root = os.path.splitdrive(str(tmp_path))[0] + "\\"
 235:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", root)
 236:     assert proc.returncode == 1 and "filesystem root" in proc.stdout, proc.stdout
 237: 
 238: 
 239: def test_a_dangling_junction_inside_the_tree_is_removed(tmp_path):
 240:     tree = tmp_path / "tree"
 241:     (tree / "sub").mkdir(parents=True)
 242:     gone = tmp_path / "gone"
 243:     gone.mkdir()
 244:     junction(tree / "sub" / "dangling", gone)
 245:     gone.rmdir()
 246:     proc = run_ps(harness(tmp_path), "-Target", str(tree))
 247:     assert proc.returncode == 0, proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 248:     assert not tree.exists()
 249: 
 250: 
 251: def test_module_is_listed_in_both_host_steps():
 252:     # The powershell-hosts job runs an EXPLICIT module list per host, and
 253:     # test_backup_lane.py records why a module left off it silently
 254:     # tested one interpreter. This module names itself in both.
 255:     workflow = read(REPO / ".github" / "workflows" / "skill-evals.yml")
 256:     rel = "evals/multi-model-verify/test_mirror_reaper.py"
 257:     for host in ("powershell.exe", "pwsh.exe"):
 258:         marker = "PARALLAX_PS_HOST: " + host
 259:         assert marker in workflow
 260:         step = workflow.split(marker, 1)[1].split("\n      - name:", 1)[0]
 261:         assert step.count(rel) == 1, rel + " must appear once in the " + host + " step"

 271: Create `tools/review-tree-removal.ps1` with exactly this content. The `Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink` body is the mirror tool's function at `tools/new-review-mirror.ps1:1791-1819`, copied VERBATIM (Task 2 deletes the original):
 272: 
 273: ```powershell
 274: # review-tree-removal.ps1 - the one removal path for a review tree, and
 275: # the directory-link guard both callers run before it.
 276: #
 277: # DOT-SOURCED, never run:
 278: #   . (Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "review-tree-removal.ps1")
 279: # from tools/new-review-mirror.ps1 (the -Force rebuild, backlog item 98)
 280: # and tools/write-attestation.ps1 (the reap after a terminal verdict,
 281: # backlog item 101). It defines functions and executes nothing else, so
 282: # dot-sourcing it has no effect until a caller calls one.
 283: #
 284: # Windows PowerShell 5.1 compatible, ASCII ONLY.
 285: 
 286: function Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink($path) {
 287:     # The path itself, then each existing ancestor up to the drive root:
 288:     # is any of them a reparse point? Attributes are read directly
 289:     # rather than after a Test-Path, because a DANGLING junction is a
 290:     # reparse point Test-Path may report as absent; a missing entry is
 291:     # the one condition that skips a level, and it is recognised by the
 292:     # exception type, never by a false from a helper. Returns the
 293:     # offending path or $null.
 294:     $probe = ([string]$path).TrimEnd("\", "/")
 295:     while ($probe -and -not [string]::IsNullOrEmpty([System.IO.Path]::GetFileName($probe))) {
 296:         $pa = 0
 297:         $missing = $false
 298:         try {
 299:             $pa = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($probe)
 300:         } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 301:             $missing = $true
 302:         } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 303:             $missing = $true
 304:         } catch {
 305:             Write-Output ("ERROR: " + $probe + " could not be read while" +
 306:                 " checking for a directory link: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 307:             exit 2
 308:         }
 309:         if (-not $missing -and (($pa -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)) {
 310:             return $probe
 311:         }
 312:         $probe = [System.IO.Path]::GetDirectoryName($probe)
 313:     }
 314:     return $null
 315: }
 316: 
 317: function Remove-ReviewTreeEntries($dir, $depth) {
 318:     # Empties $dir in post-order. Returns $null when every entry beneath
 319:     # it is gone, else the reason, which names the entry that stopped it.
 320:     #
 321:     # NEVER THROUGH A LINK. A directory link is removed with the
 322:     # non-recursive Directory.Delete and a file link with File.Delete;
 323:     # both remove the link and never its target. Measured 2026-09-13 on
 324:     # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, intact and dangling. An
 325:     # ordinary directory is emptied by this walk and then removed with
 326:     # the same non-recursive Directory.Delete, which throws when
 327:     # anything survived, so a partial removal cannot read as a whole
 328:     # one. An ordinary file has its read-only bit cleared first, because
 329:     # git marks its object files read-only and File.Delete refuses them
 330:     # as they are (measured the same day).
 331:     if ($depth -gt 128) {
 332:         return ($dir + " sits deeper than 128 levels; the walk stops" +
 333:             " rather than assume the tree ends")
 334:     }
 335:     $entries = $null
 336:     try {
 337:         $entries = [System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir)
 338:     } catch {
 339:         return ($dir + " could not be listed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 340:     }

hook: PostToolUse
hook: PostToolUse Completed
hook: PreToolUse
hook: PreToolUse Completed
exec
"C:\\Program Files\\PowerShell\\7\\pwsh.exe" -Command '$n=0; Get-Content docs/superpowers/plans/2026-09-13-mirror-reaper.md | ForEach-Object { $n++; if ($n -gt 340 -and $n -le 800) { '"'{0,4}: {1}' -f "'$n,$_ } }' in C:\Users\Brandon\AppData\Local\Temp\pxmr
 succeeded in 247ms:
 341:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
 342:         $ea = 0
 343:         try {
 344:             $ea = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
 345:         } catch {
 346:             return ($entry + " could not be examined: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 347:         }
 348:         $isDir = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -ne 0)
 349:         $isLink = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)
 350:         try {
 351:             if ($isLink) {
 352:                 if ($isDir) {
 353:                     [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
 354:                 } else {
 355:                     [System.IO.File]::Delete($entry)
 356:                 }
 357:             } elseif ($isDir) {
 358:                 $inner = Remove-ReviewTreeEntries $entry ($depth + 1)
 359:                 if ($null -ne $inner) { return $inner }
 360:                 [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
 361:             } else {
 362:                 if (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
 363:                     [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($entry, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Normal)
 364:                 }
 365:                 [System.IO.File]::Delete($entry)
 366:             }
 367:         } catch {
 368:             return ($entry + " could not be removed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 369:         }
 370:     }
 371:     return $null
 372: }
 373: 
 374: function Remove-ReviewTree($root) {
 375:     # Removes the tree at $root. Returns @{ Ok = $true }, or
 376:     # @{ Ok = $false; Reason = <text> } the moment any step fails, with
 377:     # the entry that failed named in the reason. It never exits the
 378:     # caller and never writes output, so the caller decides what a
 379:     # failure costs: the mirror tool refuses to build, the emitter
 380:     # reports a written attestation with an unreaped tree.
 381:     #
 382:     # The root's OWN guards are here; the caller runs the ancestor-link
 383:     # guard (Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink) and its overlap comparisons
 384:     # before ever reaching this, because those are refusals that must
 385:     # cost nothing, while this runs after the emitter has written.
 386:     $full = $null
 387:     try {
 388:         $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath([string]$root).TrimEnd("\")
 389:     } catch {
 390:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ("the path could not be resolved (" +
 391:             $root + "): " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 392:     }
 393:     $pathRoot = $null
 394:     try {
 395:         $pathRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($full)
 396:     } catch {
 397:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ("the path has no root (" + $full +
 398:             "): " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 399:     }
 400:     if ((-not $pathRoot) -or ($full.Length -le ([string]$pathRoot).TrimEnd("\").Length)) {
 401:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a filesystem root and" +
 402:             " is never removed") }
 403:     }
 404:     $attr = 0
 405:     try {
 406:         $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
 407:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 408:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " does not exist") }
 409:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 410:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " does not exist") }
 411:     } catch {
 412:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be examined: " +
 413:             $_.Exception.Message) }
 414:     }
 415:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
 416:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a link, and a tree is" +
 417:             " never removed through a link") }
 418:     }
 419:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
 420:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a file, not a tree") }
 421:     }
 422:     $failure = Remove-ReviewTreeEntries $full 0
 423:     if ($null -ne $failure) {
 424:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $failure }
 425:     }
 426:     try {
 427:         [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($full)
 428:     } catch {
 429:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be removed: " +
 430:             $_.Exception.Message) }
 431:     }
 432:     # THE POSTCONDITION, read back rather than inferred from the absence
 433:     # of an exception. Item 98 is a removal that checked nothing after.
 434:     try {
 435:         [void][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
 436:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 437:         return @{ Ok = $true }
 438:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 439:         return @{ Ok = $true }
 440:     } catch {
 441:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be re-examined" +
 442:             " after removal: " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 443:     }
 444:     return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " still exists after removal") }
 445: }
 446: ```

 710: @pytest.mark.parametrize("shape", ["missing", "file", "inside-repo", "repo-itself",
 711:                                    "contains-repo", "worktree", "no-git", "link"])
 712: def test_every_wrong_tree_is_refused_before_the_record_is_written(tmp_path, shape):
 713:     repo, base, head = make_repo(tmp_path)
 714:     keep = None
 715:     if shape == "missing":
 716:         path = tmp_path / "absent"
 717:     elif shape == "file":
 718:         path = tmp_path / "plain.txt"
 719:         path.write_text("x\n", encoding="utf-8")
 720:     elif shape == "inside-repo":
 721:         path = repo / "nested"
 722:         make_mirror(repo / ".git", path / ".git")
 723:     elif shape == "repo-itself":
 724:         path = repo
 725:     elif shape == "contains-repo":
 726:         path = tmp_path
 727:     elif shape == "worktree":
 728:         path = tmp_path / "wt"
 729:         git(repo, "worktree", "add", "-q", str(path), "main")
 730:         assert (path / ".git").is_file()
 731:     elif shape == "no-git":
 732:         path = tmp_path / "bare"
 733:         path.mkdir()
 734:     else:
 735:         keep = make_mirror(repo, tmp_path / "real")
 736:         path = tmp_path / "link"
 737:         junction(path, keep)
 738:     proc = attest(repo, base, head, mirror=path)
 739:     assert proc.returncode == 2, shape + ": " + proc.stdout + proc.stderr
 740:     assert proc.stdout.startswith("ERROR:"), proc.stdout
 741:     assert not att_file(repo, head).exists(), (
 742:         shape + ": a refused reap path must be refused BEFORE the record is written")
 743:     assert (repo / "b.txt").is_file()
 744:     if shape not in ("missing",):
 745:         assert path.exists(), shape + ": nothing was removed"
 746:     if keep is not None:
 747:         assert (keep / "b.txt").is_file(), "the link's target survives"

 821: (d) After the `Resolve-FullSha` function, add:
 822: 
 823: ```powershell
 824: function Resolve-ReapPath($label, $raw, $repoTop, $commonFull, $headFull, $allowRemediation) {
 825:     # Returns the resolved full path of a tree this attestation may
 826:     # remove, or prints ERROR and exits 2. Every refusal here runs before
 827:     # the record is written. The rules are the spec's identity guard:
 828:     # rooted and resolvable, not a filesystem root, an existing
 829:     # directory, not through a link, not overlapping the reviewed repo
 830:     # or its common dir, a .git DIRECTORY (a .git FILE is a linked
 831:     # worktree, never a mirror or a bridge), and a HEAD equal to the
 832:     # attested head - or, for the mirror only, one parallax@local
 833:     # remediation commit whose single parent is that head, which is the
 834:     # commit the mirror tool makes over a tracked back-channel.
 835:     if ([string]::IsNullOrWhiteSpace($raw)) {
 836:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is empty"
 837:         exit 2
 838:     }
 839:     if (-not [System.IO.Path]::IsPathRooted($raw)) {
 840:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label must be an absolute path ($raw)"
 841:         exit 2
 842:     }
 843:     $full = $null
 844:     try {
 845:         $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath($raw).TrimEnd("\")
 846:     } catch {
 847:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label could not be resolved ($raw): " + $_.Exception.Message)
 848:         exit 2
 849:     }
 850:     $pathRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($full)
 851:     if ((-not $pathRoot) -or ($full.Length -le ([string]$pathRoot).TrimEnd("\").Length)) {
 852:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is a filesystem root ($full)"
 853:         exit 2
 854:     }
 855:     $attr = 0
 856:     try {
 857:         $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
 858:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 859:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label does not exist ($full)"
 860:         exit 2
 861:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 862:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label does not exist ($full)"
 863:         exit 2
 864:     } catch {
 865:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label could not be examined ($full): " + $_.Exception.Message)
 866:         exit 2
 867:     }
 868:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
 869:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label is a file, not a tree ($full)"
 870:         exit 2
 871:     }
 872:     $hit = Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink $full
 873:     if ($hit) {
 874:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label passes through a directory link at $hit" +
 875:             " - a tree is never removed through a link")
 876:         exit 2
 877:     }
 878:     # The mirror tool's own overlap comparison, against the reviewed
 879:     # repository and against its git common dir (a linked worktree's
 880:     # common dir sits outside its top level).
 881:     $cmp = [System.StringComparison]::OrdinalIgnoreCase
 882:     $p = $full.Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 883:     foreach ($pair in @(@("the reviewed repository", $repoTop), @("the git common dir", $commonFull))) {
 884:         $g = ([string]$pair[1]).Replace("\", "/").TrimEnd("/") + "/"
 885:         if ($p.Equals($g, $cmp)) {
 886:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is " + $pair[0] + " itself ($full)")
 887:             exit 2
 888:         }
 889:         if ($p.StartsWith($g, $cmp)) {
 890:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is inside " + $pair[0] + " ($full)")
 891:             exit 2
 892:         }
 893:         if ($g.StartsWith($p, $cmp)) {
 894:             Write-Output ("ERROR: $label contains " + $pair[0] + " ($full)")
 895:             exit 2
 896:         }
 897:     }
 898:     $dotGit = Join-Path $full ".git"
 899:     $ga = 0
 900:     try {
 901:         $ga = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($dotGit)
 902:     } catch {
 903:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label carries no .git entry, so it is not a review tree ($full)"
 904:         exit 2
 905:     }
 906:     if (($ga -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
 907:         Write-Output ("ERROR: $label has a .git FILE, which marks a linked worktree" +
 908:             " - never a mirror or a bridge ($full)")
 909:         exit 2
 910:     }
 911:     $treeHead = (& git -C $full rev-parse --verify --quiet "HEAD^{commit}" 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
 912:     if (($LASTEXITCODE -ne 0) -or -not $treeHead) {
 913:         Write-Output "ERROR: $label has no readable HEAD ($full)"
 914:         exit 2
 915:     }
 916:     if ($treeHead -eq $headFull) {
 917:         return $full
 918:     }
 919:     if ($allowRemediation) {
 920:         $author = (& git -C $full log -1 --format=%ae HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()
 921:         $authorExit = $LASTEXITCODE
 922:         $parents = @(((& git -C $full rev-list --parents -n 1 HEAD 2>$null | Out-String).Trim()) -split "\s+")
 923:         if (($authorExit -eq 0) -and ($LASTEXITCODE -eq 0) -and ($author -eq "parallax@local") -and
 924:             ($parents.Count -eq 2) -and ($parents[1] -eq $headFull)) {
 925:             return $full
 926:         }
 927:     }
 928:     Write-Output ("ERROR: $label is at $treeHead, not the attested head $headFull" +
 929:         " - it is not the tree this verdict was issued on ($full)")
 930:     exit 2
 931: }
 932: 
 933: function Invoke-Reap($label, $full) {
 934:     # Runs after the record is written: a failure here is exit 3, with
 935:     # the attestation left standing and the reason named.
 936:     $r = Remove-ReviewTree $full
 937:     if (-not $r.Ok) {
 938:         Write-Output ("ERROR: reap failed for " + $full + ": " + $r.Reason +
 939:             " - the attestation stands; remove the " + $label + " by hand")
 940:         exit 3
 941:     }
 942:     Write-Output ("reaped " + $label + ": " + $full)
 943: }
 944: ```

 951:     assert not dangling, (
 952:         "prose cites region id(s) that no document declares: "
 953:         f"{dangling}. A region was renamed, split or deleted and the "
 954:         "sentence pointing at it was left behind.")
 955:     assert not wrong_file, (
 956:         "region citation(s) name the wrong file: "
 957:         f"{sorted(set(wrong_file))}. The id resolves, the pointer does "
What if: Performing the operation "Set Content" on target "Path: C:\Users\Brandon\AppData\Local\Temp\pxmr\tools\write-attestation.ps1".
What if: Performing the operation "Set Content" on target "Path: C:\Users\Brandon\AppData\Local\Temp\pxmr\tools\write-attestation.ps1".
  44: function Remove-ReviewTreeEntries($dir, $depth) {
  45:     # Empties $dir in post-order. Returns $null when every entry beneath
  46:     # it is gone, else the reason, which names the entry that stopped it.
  47:     #
  48:     # NEVER THROUGH A LINK. A directory link is removed with the
  49:     # non-recursive Directory.Delete and a file link with File.Delete;
  50:     # both remove the link and never its target. Measured 2026-09-13 on
  51:     # Windows PowerShell 5.1 and PowerShell 7, intact and dangling. An
  52:     # ordinary directory is emptied by this walk and then removed with
  53:     # the same non-recursive Directory.Delete, which throws when
  54:     # anything survived, so a partial removal cannot read as a whole
  55:     # one. An ordinary file has its read-only bit cleared first, because
  56:     # git marks its object files read-only and File.Delete refuses them
  57:     # as they are (measured the same day).
  58:     if ($depth -gt 128) {
  59:         return ($dir + " sits deeper than 128 levels; the walk stops" +
  60:             " rather than assume the tree ends")
  61:     }
  62:     $entries = $null
  63:     try {
  64:         $entries = [System.IO.Directory]::GetFileSystemEntries($dir)
  65:     } catch {
  66:         return ($dir + " could not be listed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
  67:     }
  68:     foreach ($entry in $entries) {
  69:         $ea = 0
  70:         try {
  71:             $ea = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($entry)
  72:         } catch {
  73:             return ($entry + " could not be examined: " + $_.Exception.Message)
  74:         }
  75:         $isDir = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -ne 0)
  76:         $isLink = (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0)
  77:         try {
  78:             if ($isLink) {
  79:                 if ($isDir) {
  80:                     [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
  81:                 } else {
  82:                     [System.IO.File]::Delete($entry)
  83:                 }
  84:             } elseif ($isDir) {
  85:                 $inner = Remove-ReviewTreeEntries $entry ($depth + 1)
  86:                 if ($null -ne $inner) { return $inner }
  87:                 # robocopy /DCOPY:DA carries a read-only directory into the mirror; Directory.Delete refuses it as it is (measured 2026-09-13, both hosts).
  88:                 if (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
  89:                     [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($entry, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory)
  90:                 }
  91:                 [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($entry)
  92:             } else {
  93:                 if (($ea -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
  94:                     [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($entry, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Normal)
  95:                 }
  96:                 [System.IO.File]::Delete($entry)
  97:             }
  98:         } catch {
  99:             return ($entry + " could not be removed: " + $_.Exception.Message)
 100:         }
 101:     }
 102:     return $null
 103: }
 104: 
 105: function Remove-ReviewTree($root) {
 106:     # Removes the tree at $root. Returns @{ Ok = $true }, or
 107:     # @{ Ok = $false; Reason = <text> } the moment any step fails, with
 108:     # the entry that failed named in the reason. It never exits the
 109:     # caller and never writes output, so the caller decides what a
 110:     # failure costs: the mirror tool refuses to build, the emitter
 111:     # reports a written attestation with an unreaped tree.
 112:     #
 113:     # The root's OWN guards are here; the caller runs the ancestor-link
 114:     # guard (Test-PathOrAncestorIsLink) and its overlap comparisons
 115:     # before ever reaching this, because those are refusals that must
 116:     # cost nothing, while this runs after the emitter has written.
 117:     $full = $null
 118:     try {
 119:         $full = [System.IO.Path]::GetFullPath([string]$root).TrimEnd("\")
 120:     } catch {
 121:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ("the path could not be resolved (" +
 122:             $root + "): " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 123:     }
 124:     $pathRoot = $null
 125:     try {
 126:         $pathRoot = [System.IO.Path]::GetPathRoot($full)
 127:     } catch {
 128:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ("the path has no root (" + $full +
 129:             "): " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 130:     }
 131:     if ((-not $pathRoot) -or ($full.Length -le ([string]$pathRoot).TrimEnd("\").Length)) {
 132:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a filesystem root and" +
 133:             " is never removed") }
 134:     }
 135:     $attr = 0
 136:     try {
 137:         $attr = [int][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
 138:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 139:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " does not exist") }
 140:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 141:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " does not exist") }
 142:     } catch {
 143:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be examined: " +
 144:             $_.Exception.Message) }
 145:     }
 146:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReparsePoint) -ne 0) {
 147:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a link, and a tree is" +
 148:             " never removed through a link") }
 149:     }
 150:     if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory) -eq 0) {
 151:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " is a file, not a tree") }
 152:     }
 153:     $failure = Remove-ReviewTreeEntries $full 0
 154:     if ($null -ne $failure) {
 155:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = $failure }
 156:     }
 157:     try {
 158:         # robocopy /DCOPY:DA carries a read-only directory into the mirror; Directory.Delete refuses it as it is (measured 2026-09-13, both hosts).
 159:         if (($attr -band [int][System.IO.FileAttributes]::ReadOnly) -ne 0) {
 160:             [System.IO.File]::SetAttributes($full, [System.IO.FileAttributes]::Directory)
 161:         }
 162:         [System.IO.Directory]::Delete($full)
 163:     } catch {
 164:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be removed: " +
 165:             $_.Exception.Message) }
 166:     }
 167:     # THE POSTCONDITION, read back rather than inferred from the absence
 168:     # of an exception. Item 98 is a removal that checked nothing after.
 169:     try {
 170:         [void][System.IO.File]::GetAttributes($full)
 171:     } catch [System.IO.FileNotFoundException] {
 172:         return @{ Ok = $true }
 173:     } catch [System.IO.DirectoryNotFoundException] {
 174:         return @{ Ok = $true }
 175:     } catch {
 176:         return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " could not be re-examined" +
 177:             " after removal: " + $_.Exception.Message) }
 178:     }
 179:     return @{ Ok = $false; Reason = ($full + " still exists after removal") }
 180: }
